@push('scripts')
{{-- Hapus baris jQuery ini kalau layout kaprodi kamu sudah memuat jQuery 3.x --}}
<script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.7.1.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.datatables.net/2.1.8/js/dataTables.min.js"></script>
<script>
(function () {
    var data = @json($students);
    var PAGE_SIZE = 10;   // jumlah baris per halaman

    // Halaman detail kaprodi belum dibuat: tombol aktif otomatis begitu route kaprodi.mahasiswa.show terdaftar.
    var URL_DETAIL = @js(\Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route::has('kaprodi.mahasiswa.show') ? route('kaprodi.mahasiswa.show', ['id' => '__ID__']) : null);

    var $ = function (id) { return document.getElementById(id); };

    // Nilai filter dropdown (dicek dari data asli baris)
    var filter = { level: 'all', status: 'all' };

    function esc(s) {
        return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
            return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
        });
    }
    function svg(w, sw, paths) {
        return '<svg width="' + w + '" height="' + w + '" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="' + sw + '" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">' + paths + '</svg>';
    }
    var I_EYE = svg(20, 1.5, '<path d="M10 12a2 2 0 1 0 4 0a2 2 0 0 0 -4 0" /><path d="M21 12c-2.4 4 -5.4 6 -9 6c-3.6 0 -6.6 -2 -9 -6c2.4 -4 5.4 -6 9 -6c3.6 0 6.6 2 9 6" />');

    var TXT = DataTable.render.text(); // otomatis escape HTML

    /* ---------- Filter tingkat + status ---------- */
    DataTable.ext.search.push(function (settings, searchData, dataIndex, r) {
        if (settings.nTable.id !== 'tabel') return true;
        return (filter.level === 'all' || r.level === filter.level) &&
               (filter.status === 'all' || r.status === filter.status);
    });

    var table = new DataTable('#tabel', {
        data: data,
        autoWidth: false,
        pageLength: PAGE_SIZE,
        order: [],                       // tanpa urutan awal = urutan asli dari server
        columns: [
            { data: 'nim',       render: TXT, className: 'truncate', orderable: false },   // NIM tidak bisa diurutkan (sama seperti sebelumnya)
            { data: 'name',      render: TXT, className: 'truncate' },
            { data: 'level',     render: TXT, className: 'truncate', searchable: false },
            { data: 'className', render: TXT, className: 'truncate', searchable: false },
            {
                data: 'status', searchable: false,
                render: function (d, type) {
                    if (type !== 'display') return d;
                    var warna = d === 'Aktif' ? 'border-[#D6EDB5] bg-[#E6F3D3] text-[#4C7D08]'
                              : d === 'Cuti'  ? 'border-[#FAD4A8] bg-[#FBE5CB] text-[#854700]'
                              :                 'border-[#E4E4E7] bg-[#EDEDF0] text-[#55555C]';
                    return '<span class="inline-flex rounded-lg border px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium leading-4 ' + warna + '">' + esc(d) + '</span>';
                }
            },
            {
                data: null, orderable: false, searchable: false,
                render: function (d, type, r) {
                    return '<div class="flex items-center gap-3">' +
                        '<button type="button" title="Lihat detail" aria-label="Lihat detail" data-act="view" data-id="' + esc(r.id) + '" class="rounded-md bg-[#CDDEF9] p-1.5 text-[#2277FF] hover:bg-[#BBD2F6] cursor-pointer">' + I_EYE + '</button>' +
                    '</div>';
                }
            }
        ],
        // Tooltip: arahkan kursor ke teks yang terpotong "..." -> muncul teks lengkapnya
        createdRow: function (tr, r) {
            var td = tr.children;
            td[0].title = r.nim || '';
            td[1].title = r.name || '';
        },
        // Hanya paginasi (tengah bawah). Kotak cari, "show entries", dan info bawaan dimatikan
        layout: { topStart: null, topEnd: null, bottomStart: null, bottomEnd: null, bottom: 'paging' },
        pagingType: 'simple_numbers',
        language: {
            emptyTable: 'Tidak ada data',
            zeroRecords: 'Tidak ada data',
            paginate: { previous: 'Sebelumnya', next: 'Berikutnya' }
        }
    });

    // Sembunyikan paginasi kalau hanya ada 1 halaman
    function togglePager() {
        var p = document.querySelector('#tabel-wrap .dt-paging');
        if (p) p.style.display = table.page.info().pages > 1 ? '' : 'none';
    }
    table.on('draw.dt', togglePager);
    togglePager();

    /* ---------- Event filter ---------- */
    // Cari di NIM dan nama (kolom lain tidak ikut dicari)
    $('q').addEventListener('input', function (e) { table.search(e.target.value.trim()).draw(); });
    $('fLevel').addEventListener('change', function (e) { filter.level = e.target.value; table.draw(); });
    $('fStatus').addEventListener('change', function (e) { filter.status = e.target.value; table.draw(); });

    /* ---------- Aksi: lihat detail (aktif bila route kaprodi.mahasiswa.show ada) ---------- */
    $('tabel').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
        var b = e.target.closest('button[data-act]'); if (!b) return;
        if (b.dataset.act === 'view' && URL_DETAIL) {
            window.location.href = URL_DETAIL.replace('__ID__', encodeURIComponent(b.dataset.id));
        }
    });
})();
</script>
@endpush

@push('scripts')
<script>
(function () {
    var tabs = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-tab]'));
    var panels = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll('[data-panel]'));

    function show(key, push) {
        tabs.forEach(function (t) { t.setAttribute('aria-selected', t.dataset.tab === key ? 'true' : 'false'); });
        panels.forEach(function (p) { p.classList.toggle('hidden', p.dataset.panel !== key); });
        if (push) history.replaceState(null, '', '?tab=' + key);
    }

    tabs.forEach(function (t) {
        t.addEventListener('click', function () { show(t.dataset.tab, true); });
    });
})();
</script>
@endpush

    <script>
        // Skala panel otomatis: lebar kerja panel dibuat setara 1440px di semua ukuran laptop.
        (function () {
            var BASE = 1440, MIN = 0.75, MAX = 1.6;   // ubah BASE untuk memperbesar/memperkecil semua halaman
            function skala() {
                var w = window.innerWidth;
                document.documentElement.style.zoom = w < 1024 ? 1 : Math.min(MAX, Math.max(MIN, w / BASE));
            }
            skala();
            window.addEventListener('resize', skala);
        })();
    </script>

===== resources\views\mahasiswa\mahasiswa\pagination\mahasiswa.blade.php =====

@if ($paginator->hasPages())
    @php
        $nav = 'flex size-9 items-center justify-center rounded-md text-base font-medium';
    @endphp
    <nav role="navigation" aria-label="Navigasi halaman" class="flex justify-center">
        <ul class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <li>
                @if ($paginator->onFirstPage())
                    <span class="{{ $nav }} cursor-not-allowed bg-[#E5E7EB] text-[#A8A8A8]" aria-disabled="true" aria-label="Sebelumnya">
                        <svg class="size-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M15 6l-6 6l6 6"/></svg>
                    </span>
                @else
                    <a href="{{ $paginator->previousPageUrl() }}" rel="prev" aria-label="Sebelumnya" class="{{ $nav }} bg-[#27F] text-white">
                        <svg class="size-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M15 6l-6 6l6 6"/></svg>
                    </a>
                @endif
            </li>

            @foreach ($elements as $element)
                @if (is_string($element))
                    <li><span class="{{ $nav }} border border-[#E5E7EB] text-[#0A0A0A]">â€¦</span></li>
                @endif

                @if (is_array($element))
                    @foreach ($element as $page => $url)
                        <li>
                            @if ($page == $paginator->currentPage())
                                <span aria-current="page" class="{{ $nav }} border border-[#27F] text-[#0162DD]">{{ $page }}</span>
                            @else
                                <a href="{{ $url }}" aria-label="Halaman {{ $page }}" class="{{ $nav }} border border-[#E5E7EB] text-[#0A0A0A] hover:border-[#27F]">{{ $page }}</a>
                            @endif
                        </li>
                    @endforeach
                @endif
            @endforeach

            <li>
                @if ($paginator->hasMorePages())
                    <a href="{{ $paginator->nextPageUrl() }}" rel="next" aria-label="Berikutnya" class="{{ $nav }} border border-[#27F] bg-[#DCE7FB] text-[#0162DD]">
                        <svg class="size-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M9 6l6 6l-6 6"/></svg>
                    </a>
                @else
                    <span class="{{ $nav }} cursor-not-allowed bg-[#E5E7EB] text-[#A8A8A8]" aria-disabled="true" aria-label="Berikutnya">
                        <svg class="size-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M9 6l6 6l-6 6"/></svg>
                    </span>
                @endif
            </li>
        </ul>
    </nav>
@endif

@extends('layouts.app')

@section('title', 'Pengajuan Dosen')

@php
    // DATA TIRUAN. Nanti diganti data dari controller / database oleh tim backend.
    // status: Diajukan | Direview | Direvisi | Ditolak | Diterima | Selesai
    $daftar = [
        ['judul' => 'Penyuluhan Literasi Digital', 'oleh' => 'Filda Huwani Dewi',  'kategori' => 'Pengabdian', 'tanggal' => '3 Oktober 2026',  'status' => 'Diajukan', 'catatan' => null],
        ['judul' => 'Seminar Nasional AI',         'oleh' => 'Angga Wahyu Wibowo', 'kategori' => 'Pengabdian', 'tanggal' => '15 Agustus 2026', 'status' => 'Direview', 'catatan' => null],
        ['judul' => 'Pelatihan Industri',          'oleh' => 'Kuwat Santoso',      'kategori' => 'Pengabdian', 'tanggal' => '12 Agustus 2026', 'status' => 'Direvisi', 'catatan' => 'Lampirkan surat tugas yang sudah ditandatangani.'],
        ['judul' => 'Woorkshop Penulisan Jurnal',  'oleh' => 'Tahan Prahara',      'kategori' => 'Pengabdian', 'tanggal' => '2 Juli 2026',     'status' => 'Ditolak',  'catatan' => 'Jadwal bentrok dengan kegiatan fakultas pada tanggal yang sama.'],
        ['judul' => 'Kunjungan Industri',          'oleh' => 'Wiktasari',          'kategori' => 'Pengabdian', 'tanggal' => '26 Juli 2026',    'status' => 'Diterima', 'catatan' => null],
        ['judul' => 'Kuliah Tamu',                 'oleh' => 'Kuwat Santoso',      'kategori' => 'Pengabdian', 'tanggal' => '10 Juli 2026',    'status' => 'Selesai',  'catatan' => null],
    ];

    // Status => keadaan 4 langkah (Diajukan, Direview, Keputusan, Selesai).
    // d = sudah lewat (centang biru), a = sedang berjalan, m = belum, h = selesai hijau
    $alur = [
        'Diajukan' => ['langkah' => ['a', 'm', 'm', 'm'], 'warna' => 'biru',   'label3' => 'Keputusan'],
        'Direview' => ['langkah' => ['d', 'a', 'm', 'm'], 'warna' => 'biru',   'label3' => 'Keputusan'],
        'Direvisi' => ['langkah' => ['d', 'd', 'a', 'm'], 'warna' => 'oranye', 'label3' => 'Direvisi'],
        'Ditolak'  => ['langkah' => ['d', 'd', 'a', 'm'], 'warna' => 'merah',  'label3' => 'Ditolak'],
        'Diterima' => ['langkah' => ['d', 'd', 'h', 'm'], 'warna' => 'hijau',  'label3' => 'Diterima'],
        'Selesai'  => ['langkah' => ['d', 'd', 'd', 'h'], 'warna' => 'hijau',  'label3' => 'Diterima'],
    ];
    $daftar = array_map(function ($k) use ($alur) {
        $a = $alur[$k['status']];
        $k['langkah'] = $a['langkah'];
        $k['warna']   = $a['warna'];
        $k['label3']  = $a['label3'];
        return $k;
    }, $daftar);
    // id tetap (1, 2, 3, ...) supaya tombol Tinjau tidak berubah saat daftar difilter oleh tab atau pencarian.
    foreach ($daftar as $n => &$d) { $d['id'] = $n + 1; }
    unset($d);
@endphp

@section('content')

    <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Pengajuan Dosen</h1>
        <p class="text-2xl text-gray-400">Dashboard / Pengajuan / Dosen</p>
    </div>

    <section class="flex flex-col gap-6 rounded-lg bg-white p-4 shadow-sm"
             x-data="pengajuanDosen(@js($daftar))">

        {{-- Tab status. Angka biru hanya tampil di tab yang aktif. --}}
        <div role="tablist" aria-label="Status pengajuan" class="flex gap-x-2 overflow-x-auto border-b border-gray-200 pb-px [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden">
            <template x-for="t in tab" :key="t">
                <button type="button" role="tab" :aria-selected="aktif === t" x-on:click="aktif = t"
                        :class="aktif === t ? 'border-brand text-gray-900' : 'border-transparent text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-700'"
                        class="-mb-px flex shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm">
                    <span x-text="t"></span>
                    <span x-show="aktif === t" x-cloak
                          class="flex h-5 min-w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-brand px-1.5 text-xs text-white"
                          x-text="jumlah(t)"></span>
                </button>
            </template>
        </div>

        {{-- Pencarian --}}
        <div class="relative">
            <label for="q" class="sr-only">Cari kegiatan atau nama dosen</label>
            <svg class="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-3 size-5 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7"/><path d="M16.5 16.5L21 21"/></svg>
            <input id="q" type="search" x-model="cari" placeholder="Cari kegiatan atau nama dosen..."
                   class="h-10 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white pr-3 pl-10 text-[15px] placeholder:text-gray-400">
        </div>

        {{-- Daftar pengajuan --}}
        <div class="flex flex-col gap-5">
            <template x-for="(k, i) in tampil" :key="k.judul">
                <article class="flex flex-col gap-5 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 p-4">

                    <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                        <div class="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-2 text-[15px]">
                            <h2 class="text-lg font-semibold" x-text="k.judul"></h2>
                            <p>Diajukan oleh <span class="font-semibold" x-text="k.oleh"></span></p>
                            <p><span x-text="k.kategori"></span> &middot; Diajukan <span x-text="k.tanggal"></span></p>
                        </div>

                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {{-- Alur 4 langkah --}}
                    <ol class="flex" aria-label="Tahapan pengajuan">
                        <template x-for="(s, n) in k.langkah" :key="n">
                            <li class="flex flex-col items-start gap-2" :class="n === 3 ? 'shrink-0' : 'flex-1'">
                                <div class="flex w-full items-center">
                                    <span :class="lingkaran(k, n)"
                                          class="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 text-xs font-medium">
                                        <template x-if="s === 'd' || s === 'h'">
                                            <svg class="size-4" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12.5l4.5 4.5L19 7.5"/></svg>
                                        </template>
                                        <template x-if="s === 'a' || s === 'm'">
                                            <span x-text="'0' + (n + 1)"></span>
                                        </template>
                                    </span>
                                    <span x-show="n < 3" class="mx-2 h-px flex-1 bg-gray-400" aria-hidden="true"></span>
                                </div>
                                <span :class="teksLangkah(k, n)" class="text-xs font-medium" x-text="namaLangkah(k, n)"></span>
                            </li>
                        </template>
                    </ol>

                    {{-- Catatan reviewer (hanya kalau ada: Direvisi / Ditolak) --}}
                    <template x-if="k.catatan">
                        <div :class="k.status === 'Ditolak' ? 'bg-red-400' : 'bg-orange-300'" class="rounded-md px-3 py-2.5 text-gray-900">
                            <p class="text-xs font-semibold">Catatan reviewer</p>
                            <p class="text-xs" x-text="k.catatan"></p>
                        </div>
                    </template>
                </article>
            </template>

            <p x-show="tampil.length === 0" x-cloak class="px-4 py-10 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
                Tidak ada pengajuan yang cocok dengan filter ini.
            </p>
        </div>
    </section>

@endsection

@push('scripts')
<script>
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {
        Alpine.data('pengajuanDosen', (daftar) => ({
            daftar,
            tab: ['Semua', 'Diajukan', 'Direview', 'Direvisi', 'Ditolak', 'Diterima', 'Selesai'],
            aktif: 'Semua',
            cari: '',

            warnaBadge: {
                Diajukan: 'border border-blue-200 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',
                Direview: 'border border-blue-200 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',
                Direvisi: 'border border-orange-200 bg-orange-100 text-orange-700',
                Ditolak:  'border border-red-200 bg-red-100 text-red-600',
                Diterima: 'border border-green-200 bg-green-100 text-green-700',
                Selesai:  'border border-green-200 bg-green-100 text-green-700',
            },

            jumlah(t) {
                return t === 'Semua' ? this.daftar.length : this.daftar.filter(k => k.status === t).length;
            },

            get tampil() {
                const q = this.cari.trim().toLowerCase();
                return this.daftar.filter(k =>
                    (this.aktif === 'Semua' || k.status === this.aktif) &&
                    (q === '' || k.judul.toLowerCase().includes(q) || k.oleh.toLowerCase().includes(q))
                );
            },

            // Tampilan langkah. s: d = lewat, a = berjalan, h = selesai hijau, m = belum
            warnaAktif(k, n) {
                // langkah 3 yang sedang berjalan ikut warna status (oranye / merah)
                if (n === 2 && k.langkah[2] === 'a') {
                    return { oranye: 'orange', merah: 'red' }[k.warna] || 'blue';
                }
                return 'blue';
            },
            lingkaran(k, n) {
                const s = k.langkah[n];
                if (s === 'd') return 'border-brand bg-brand text-white';
                if (s === 'h') return 'border-lime-500 bg-lime-500 text-white';
                if (s === 'a') {
                    const w = this.warnaAktif(k, n);
                    return w === 'orange' ? 'border-orange-400 bg-white text-orange-400'
                         : w === 'red'    ? 'border-red-500 bg-white text-red-500'
                         :                  'border-brand bg-white text-brand';
                }
                return 'border-gray-400 bg-white text-gray-700';
            },
            teksLangkah(k, n) {
                const s = k.langkah[n];
                if (s === 'm') return 'text-gray-700';
                if (s === 'h') return 'text-lime-600';
                if (s === 'a') {
                    const w = this.warnaAktif(k, n);
                    return w === 'orange' ? 'text-orange-400' : w === 'red' ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-brand';
                }
                return 'text-brand';
            },
            namaLangkah(k, n) {
                return ['Diajukan', 'Direview', k.label3, 'Selesai'][n];
            },
        }));
    });
</script>
@endpush

@push('scripts')
<script>
    document.addEventListener('alpine:init', () => {
        Alpine.data('pengajuanMahasiswa', (daftar, dosen) => ({
            daftar, dosen,
            tab: ['Semua', 'Diajukan', 'Direview', 'Direvisi', 'Ditolak', 'Diterima', 'Selesai'],
            aktif: 'Semua',
            cari: '',

            warnaBadge: {
                Diajukan: 'border border-blue-200 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',
                Direview: 'border border-blue-200 bg-blue-100 text-blue-700',
                Direvisi: 'border border-orange-200 bg-orange-100 text-orange-700',
                Ditolak:  'border border-red-200 bg-red-100 text-red-600',
                Diterima: 'border border-green-200 bg-green-100 text-green-700',
                Selesai:  'border border-green-200 bg-green-100 text-green-700',
            },

            jumlah(t) {
                return t === 'Semua' ? this.daftar.length : this.daftar.filter(k => k.status === t).length;
            },

            get tampil() {
                const q = this.cari.trim().toLowerCase();
                return this.daftar.filter(k =>
                    (this.aktif === 'Semua' || k.status === this.aktif) &&
                    (q === '' || k.judul.toLowerCase().includes(q) || k.oleh.toLowerCase().includes(q) || this.dosen.toLowerCase().includes(q))
                );
            },

            // Tampilan langkah. s: d = lewat, a = berjalan, h = selesai hijau, m = belum
            warnaAktif(k, n) {
                // langkah 3 yang sedang berjalan ikut warna status (oranye / merah)
                if (n === 2 && k.langkah[2] === 'a') {
                    return { oranye: 'orange', merah: 'red' }[k.warna] || 'blue';
                }
                return 'blue';
            },
            lingkaran(k, n) {
                const s = k.langkah[n];
                if (s === 'd') return 'border-brand bg-brand text-white';
                if (s === 'h') return 'border-lime-500 bg-lime-500 text-white';
                if (s === 'a') {
                    const w = this.warnaAktif(k, n);
                    return w === 'orange' ? 'border-orange-400 bg-white text-orange-400'
                         : w === 'red'    ? 'border-red-500 bg-white text-red-500'
                         :                  'border-brand bg-white text-brand';
                }
                return 'border-gray-400 bg-white text-gray-700';
            },
            teksLangkah(k, n) {
                const s = k.langkah[n];
                if (s === 'm') return 'text-gray-700';
                if (s === 'h') return 'text-lime-600';
                if (s === 'a') {
                    const w = this.warnaAktif(k, n);
                    return w === 'orange' ? 'text-orange-400' : w === 'red' ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-brand';
                }
                return 'text-brand';
            },
            namaLangkah(k, n) {
                return ['Diajukan', 'Direview', k.label3, 'Selesai'][n];
            },
        }));
    });
</script>
@endpush

        'admin.prestasi.create',
        'admin.prestasi.show',
        'admin.prestasi.edit',
    ];

    foreach (config('admin.menu') as $m) {
        foreach ($m['children'] ?? [$m] as $c) {
            if (($c['route'] ?? null) && ! in_array($c['route'], $sudahAda)) {
                Route::get($c['url'], [LandingDashboardController::class, 'placeholder'])->name($c['route']);
            }
        }
    }
});

        'kaprodi.kegiatan.dosen',
        'kaprodi.kegiatan.mahasiswa',
        'kaprodi.kegiatan.kategori-kegiatan',
        'kaprodi.kegiatan.jenis-dokumen',
        'kaprodi.berita',
        'kaprodi.pengajuan',
        'kaprodi.pengajuan.show',
        'kaprodi.prestasi',
        'kaprodi.profil',
    ];

    foreach (config('kaprodi.menu') as $m) {
        foreach ($m['children'] ?? [$m] as $c) {
            if (($c['route'] ?? null) && ! in_array($c['route'], $sudahAdaKaprodi)) {
                Route::get($c['url'], [KaprodiDashboardController::class, 'placeholder'])->name($c['route']);
            }
        }
    }
});


===== routes\mahasiswa.php =====

<?php

use App\Http\Controllers\MahasiswaController;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

/*
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| Routes Mahasiswa
|--------------------------------------------------------------------------
| View yang dipakai (resources/views/mahasiswa/):
|   dashboard-mhs.blade.php, mata-kuliah.blade.php,
|   prestasi.blade.php, prestasi-tambah.blade.php
|
| Semua URL diawali /mahasiswa dan semua nama route diawali "mahasiswa.".
| Jika perlu login, tambahkan ->middleware('auth') pada Route::prefix di bawah.
*/

Route::prefix('mahasiswa')->group(function () {

    // /mahasiswa -> langsung ke dashboard mahasiswa
    Route::redirect('/', '/mahasiswa/dashboard')->name('mahasiswa.index');

    // ----- Dashboard -----
    Route::get('/dashboard', [MahasiswaController::class, 'dashboard'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.dashboard');

    // ----- Mata kuliah (3 tab; tiap tab punya route sendiri supaya tombol tab bisa berpindah halaman) -----
    // Menu sidebar "Mata kuliah" -> langsung membuka tab "Semester berjalan"
    Route::get('/mata-kuliah', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])
        ->defaults('tab', 'berjalan')
        ->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah');

    // Tab "Semester berjalan"
    Route::get('/mata-kuliah/semester-berjalan', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])
        ->defaults('tab', 'berjalan')
        ->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah.berjalan');

    // Tab "Riwayat mata kuliah"
    Route::get('/mata-kuliah/riwayat', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])
        ->defaults('tab', 'semua')
        ->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah.riwayat');

    // Tab "Semua mata kuliah" (Tingkat 1 - 4)
    Route::get('/mata-kuliah/katalog', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])
        ->defaults('tab', 'katalog')
        ->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah.katalog');

    // ----- Prestasi -----
    Route::get('/prestasi', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasi'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.prestasi');

    // "tambah" harus di atas route yang memakai {prestasi}
    Route::get('/prestasi/tambah', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiTambah'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.prestasi.tambah');

    Route::post('/prestasi', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiSimpan'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.prestasi.simpan');

    Route::get('/prestasi/{prestasi}/edit', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiEdit'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.prestasi.edit');

    Route::put('/prestasi/{prestasi}', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiUpdate'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.prestasi.update');

    Route::delete('/prestasi/{prestasi}', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiHapus'])
        ->name('mahasiswa.prestasi.hapus');
});


===== routes\web.php =====

<?php

use App\BackendKel3\Dashboard\DosenDashboardController;
use App\Http\Controllers\DashboardController;
use App\Http\Controllers\HomeController;
use App\Http\Controllers\KegiatanController;
use App\Http\Controllers\MahasiswaController;
use App\Http\Controllers\PengajuanController;
use App\Http\Middleware\MahasiswaOnly;
use Illuminate\Support\Facades\Route;

// Landing Page TRK
Route::get('/', [HomeController::class, 'index'])->name('home');

Route::redirect('/dashboard', '/mahasiswa/dashboard')->name('dashboard');
Route::get('/dosen/dashboard', [DosenDashboardController::class, 'index'])->name('dosen.dashboard');

Route::get('/dosen/kegiatan', [KegiatanController::class, 'index'])->name('dosen.kegiatan.index');
Route::view('/dosen/kegiatan/create', 'dosen.kegiatan.create')->name('dosen.kegiatan.create');
Route::get('/dosen/kegiatan/{id}', [KegiatanController::class, 'show'])->whereNumber('id')->name('dosen.kegiatan.show');
Route::view('/dosen/kegiatan/{id}/edit', 'dosen.kegiatan.edit')->whereNumber('id')->name('dosen.kegiatan.edit');

Route::view('/prestasi', 'dosen.prestasi.index')->name('prestasi.index');
Route::view('/prestasi/create', 'dosen.prestasi.create')->name('prestasi.create');
Route::view('/prestasi/{id}', 'dosen.prestasi.show')->whereNumber('id')->name('prestasi.show');
Route::view('/prestasi/{id}/edit', 'dosen.prestasi.edit')->whereNumber('id')->name('prestasi.edit');
Route::view('/verifikasi/{id}', 'pengajuan.show', ['asal' => 'dosen'])->whereNumber('id')->name('verifikasi.show');

Route::view('/profil', 'dosen.profil.show')->name('profil');
Route::view('/notifikasi', 'dosen.notifikasi.index')->name('notifikasi');

Route::view('/penelitian', 'dosen.penelitian.index')->name('penelitian.index');

Route::view('/penelitian/{id}/edit', 'dosen.penelitian.edit')->whereNumber('id')->name('penelitian.edit');

Route::view('/pengabdian/{id}', 'dosen.pengabdian.show')->whereNumber('id')->name('pengabdian.show');
Route::view('/pengabdian/{id}/edit', 'dosen.pengabdian.edit')->whereNumber('id')->name('pengabdian.edit');

// KODE BARU (Menggunakan Route::view karena data diambil via API):

Route::view('/pengajaran/create', 'dosen.pengajaran.create')->name('pengajaran.create');
Route::view('/pengajaran/{id}', 'dosen.pengajaran.show')->whereNumber('id')->name('pengajaran.show');
Route::view('/pengajaran/{id}/edit', 'dosen.pengajaran.edit')->whereNumber('id')->name('pengajaran.edit');

Route::redirect('/kegiatan/create', '/mahasiswa/kegiatan/create');
Route::middleware(MahasiswaOnly::class)->group(function () {
    Route::get('/mahasiswa/kegiatan', [KegiatanController::class, 'index'])->name('kegiatan.index');
    Route::get('/mahasiswa/kegiatan/create', [PengajuanController::class, 'createActivity'])->name('kegiatan.create');
    Route::post('/mahasiswa/kegiatan', [PengajuanController::class, 'storeActivity'])->name('kegiatan.store');

    // Mengirimkan semua variabel dummy yang dibutuhkan kegiatan.blade.php
    Route::view('/mahasiswa/kegiatan/{id}/edit', 'kegiatan', [
        'allActivities' => [],
        'categories' => [],
        'activities' => [],
    ])->whereNumber('id')->name('kegiatan.edit');

    Route::get('/mahasiswa/kegiatan/{id}', [KegiatanController::class, 'show'])->whereNumber('id')->name('kegiatan.show');
});

    ->middleware(MahasiswaOnly::class)
    ->name('pengajuan.dashboard');

    ->middleware(MahasiswaOnly::class)
    ->name('mahasiswa.index');

    ->middleware(MahasiswaOnly::class)
    ->name('mahasiswa.dashboard');
Route::middleware(MahasiswaOnly::class)->group(function () {
    Route::get('/mahasiswa/mata-kuliah', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])->defaults('tab', 'berjalan')->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah');
    Route::get('/mahasiswa/mata-kuliah/semester-berjalan', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])->defaults('tab', 'berjalan')->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah.berjalan');
    Route::get('/mahasiswa/mata-kuliah/katalog', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])->defaults('tab', 'katalog')->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah.katalog');
    Route::get('/mahasiswa/mata-kuliah/riwayat', [MahasiswaController::class, 'mataKuliah'])->defaults('tab', 'semua')->name('mahasiswa.mata-kuliah.riwayat');
    Route::view('/mahasiswa/profil', 'mahasiswa.mahasiswa.profil')->name('mahasiswa.profil');
    Route::prefix('mahasiswa')->name('mahasiswa.')->group(function () {
        Route::get('/prestasi', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasi'])
            ->name('prestasi');

        // Letakkan route statis sebelum route parameter prestasi.
        Route::get('/prestasi/tambah', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiTambah'])
            ->name('prestasi.tambah');
        Route::post('/prestasi', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiSimpan'])
            ->name('prestasi.simpan');

        Route::get('/prestasi/{prestasi}/edit', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiEdit'])
            ->name('prestasi.edit');
        Route::put('/prestasi/{prestasi}', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiUpdate'])
            ->name('prestasi.update');
        Route::delete('/prestasi/{prestasi}', [MahasiswaController::class, 'prestasiHapus'])
            ->name('prestasi.hapus');
    });
});
